# TH09 verified knowledge

This file stores durable TH09-scoped facts and important negative results.
Cross-game promotion is a later Factory review, never an automatic action from
this repository.

## Packet 175 CardAttack knowledge

- `CardAttackInf` is a TH09-local registry anchor for a per-side authored owner, not a direct transplant of the TH08/TH095 classic `Spellcard` object. TH09 `CardAttack::Create @ 0x00404600` allocates `0x1368` bytes per side, while the consulted adjacent classic Spellcard family uses a materially different approximately `0x2644` layout. Adjacent source is therefore naming/lifecycle hypothesis material only.
- Reviewed CardAttack owner functions are `RunEndCallbacks @ 0x00403D60` (31), `RefreshResource @ 0x00403D80` (12), `OnUpdate @ 0x004041F0` (536), `OnDraw @ 0x00404410` (334), `AddedCallback @ 0x00404560` (8), `Destroy @ 0x00404570` (51), constructor `@ 0x004045B0` (66), and `Create @ 0x00404600` (264). The owned function bytes total 1,302; observed `CC` alignment between several owners remains unowned.
- The target-backed owner layout is size `0x1368`: side index `+0x00`, side/opponent state pointers `+0x04/+0x08`, calc/draw chains `+0x0C/+0x10`, presentation `AnmLoaded *` `+0x14`, `ZunTimer +0x98`, presentation counters `+0xA4/+0xA8`, two `0x2A4` AnmVm objects at `+0x0AC`, five at `+0x5F4`, and update/draw/end callback slots at `+0x1328..+0x133C`. The remainder and original source identifiers stay unknown.
- `Create` binds 0x38-stride side/opponent state records, installs `OnUpdate` on the calc chain at priority `side+17`, installs `OnDraw` on the draw chain at priority `side+24`, and returns null only when calc-chain insertion fails. `Destroy` runs the two end callbacks, cuts both chain elements, and frees the owner.
- Repository-defined pinned VC7.1 replays are exact for six CardAttack functions: `RunEndCallbacks` 31/31, `RefreshResource` 12/12, `AddedCallback` 8/8, `Destroy` 51/51, constructor 66/66, and `Create` 264/264. The constructor requires a function-focused `/O2 /Ob0` profile to preserve VC7.1 vector-constructor calls; the other five use the established `/O2 /Ob1` profile. This is not a TU- or project-wide flag claim.
- `OnUpdate` and `OnDraw` are maintained natural source but remain NON-EXACT. The current pinned `/O2 /Ob1` objects are 520 versus 536 target bytes and 324 versus 334 target bytes respectively. Their target-observed behavior/layout is retained without register forcing, inert padding, assembly, or target-byte embedding.
